- **agentize-websites**: Install, configure, deploy, verify, or troubleshoot Agentize in website-owner repositories to expose first-party semantic search under /agents/*. Use for Agentize integrations, not arbitrary-site crawling or general SEO search. ([nicolasakf/agentize](https://github.com/nicolasakf/agentize/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agentize-websites) · ★3)
- **agentizer**: Make any text or conversation sound MORE like AI — not less. A satirical reverse-humanizer that injects maximum AI-isms (em dashes, rule of three, sycophantic openers, tech metaphors, philosophical jargon, open-ended closers) for comedy and critique. Supports /agentizer-on, /agentizer-off for persistent conversation mode and /agentizer [text] for one-shot rewrites. ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agentizer) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **agentizer-zh**: 让任何文本或对话更像 AI —— 不是去 AI 味，而是把 AI 味拉满。一个反讽性质的 逆向去痕工具，注入最浓的 AI 八股文（否定排比、三段式、科技隐喻、抽象哲学、 开放结尾）用于搞笑和批判。支持 /agentizer-on、/agentizer-off 持续对话模式， 以及 /agentizer [文本] 单次改写。 ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agentizer-zh) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **agentizzy-phone-agent**: AI phone agent for any business — answers inbound calls 24/7, captures leads, books appointments, and returns structured call data with transcripts, summaries, and sentiment. Provision a phone agent in seconds via API, then query calls, leads, and analytics. Use to add phone handling to any agent workflow. ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agentizzy-phone-agent) · ★10)

Используй, когда пользователь говорит «длинная сессия», «поставь канарейку», «следи, чтобы ты не поплыл», «разбор инцидента надолго», «контекст скоро кончится», «ты начал забывать инструкции».
